  We investigate whether a lightweight vision-language model can serve as an effective critic for generated interfaces. We unify 19 interface-quality principles from three complementary sources of HCI knowledge: WCAG 2.2 accessibility standards, deceptive design taxonomies, and established theories of perception, cognition, and interaction. To train this critic, we construct a verified dataset of approximately 10,000 generated web pages by synthetically injecting known violations into clean, LLM-generated Tailwind pages.
  Continued reinforcement learning on a 4B vision-language model improves micro-F1 from 36\% to 84\%, with 13 of 19 principles exceeding 80\% F1. The resulting critic can audit generated interfaces, filter low-quality interface training data, and provide a reward signal for design-aware code generation. We release our data-generation recipe and injection/verification prompts to support reproducible evaluation and future work on scalable interface-quality assessment.

</details>


### [136] [REGARD: Regional Affective Differences in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20722)
*Andrei Chetvergov,Alexander Evseev,Mikhail Solovev,Timofei Sivoraksha,Stepan Ukolov,Valeriia Kuschenko,Maria Chistyakova,Sergey Bolovtsov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出REGARD框架，利用效价-唤醒-支配（VAD）剖面分析19个大语言模型对后苏联实体情感框架的差异，发现模型分为三个行为集群，通用回答率与低唤醒度强相关，VAD能捕捉传统情感分析忽略的情绪强度维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统情感分析仅用单一正负轴评估LLM对政治文化实体的态度，忽略了情绪强度等维度。作者希望引入VAD（效价-唤醒-支配）剖面，更全面地捕捉不同语言生态下LLM的情感框架差异。

Method: 选取19个LLM，针对500个后苏联相关目标实体进行查询，使用GPT-4o-mini和Qwen3.6-35B-A3B两个独立LLM评判器对响应评分，并在300个人工标注子集上验证。对模型的情感与响应行为剖面进行Ward链接聚类分析。

Result: 聚类得到三个行为集群，跨越模型来源、系列和参数量。通用回答率与唤醒度显著负相关（r=-0.81），使用模板化回应的模型无论来源如何都聚集在低唤醒度集群。

Conclusion: VAD剖面能够捕获传统情感评价无法识别的情绪强度维度，为理解LLM在不同语言生态中的情感框架差异提供了更丰富的视角。

Abstract: Large language models trained and aligned within different linguistic and regional ecosystems may frame the same political, cultural, and geopolitical entities in different ways. Such differences are often evaluated through sentiment, favorability, or stance, reducing model attitudes to a single positive-negative axis. We introduce REGARD, a study of what drives affective framing differences across LLMs on post-Soviet entities using target-directed Valence-Arousal-Dominance profiling. We query 19 models on 500 region-specific targets, score their responses with two independent LLM judges, GPT-4o-mini and Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, and validate the measurements on a 300-item human-annotated subset. Post-hoc Ward-linkage clustering of all 19 models by affective and response-behavior profiles yields three behavioral clusters that cut across model origin, family, and parameter count. Generic-answer rate is strongly associated with lower arousal (r = -0.81) and with cluster placement: models that deflect evaluative prompts with templated responses cluster together at low arousal regardless of origin. These findings show that VAD profiling captures emotional intensity, a dimension of affective framing that is largely invisible to conventional sentiment-based evaluation.

</details>


### [137] [Rushes: A Human Preference Dataset for Pluralistic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20767)
*Michael Xu,Jorge Leandro,Sudha Rao,Weijia Xu,Nebojsa Jojic,Gabriel DesGarennes,Chris Quirk,Bill Dolan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了 Rushes，一个用于研究交互式叙事环境中人类参与偏好的数据集和基准。包含44,226个决策事件，来自8,167名用户。实验发现前沿LLM（如GPT-5）在预测用户选择上甚至不如简单的流行度基线，体现了“参与度差距”，表明单一的人口层面目标（如RLHF）难以捕捉异质、上下文依赖的参与信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的大语言模型对齐方法（如RLHF）依赖于单一的人口层面目标，难以捕捉个体化、上下文相关的参与信号。缺乏一个能够诊断模型在多元化对齐（pluralistic alignment）方面表现的基准，尤其是在交互式叙事环境中，用户的选择具有时间序列和个人化特点。

Method: 通过游戏界面收集数据：用户与AI生成的分支叙事互动，在每个决策点从一个小型显式候选集中选择一个选项。记录候选集、用户选择、叙事上下文，形成带时间顺序的轨迹和持久性用户标识。数据集包含6个游戏，44,226个决策事件，8,167名唯一用户。使用选择熵量化用户行为的结构化程度，并评估多种模型（包括经典矩阵分解SVD和前沿LLM）在事件级选择预测上的表现。

Result: 用户选择表现出结构化的非随机模式（低选择熵）。SVD能捕捉一定个性化信号（37.7%），但前沿LLM（GPT-5等）仅达34.23%，甚至低于流行度基线（36.4%），揭示了明显的“参与度差距”。这表明当前LLM在适应个体轨迹方面存在不足。

Conclusion: Rushes 数据集和基准有助于研究多元化对齐和生成系统中的序列决策。当前的RLHF等方法不足以处理异质的参与信号，需要新的方法来捕捉个人化的偏好。数据集和代码已开源。

Abstract: We introduce Rushes, a dataset and benchmark for studying revealed human engagement preferences in interactive narrative environments. Rushes is collected through a game interface where users interact with AI-generated branching narratives and select one choice from a small, explicit candidate set at each decision point. Each interaction logs the full candidate set, the user's choice, and the evolving narrative context, yielding time-ordered trajectories with persistent user-level identifiers. Rushes contains 44,226 decision events from 8,167 unique users across six games, capturing sequential, personalized engagement behavior rather than static judgments. We show that user choices exhibit structured, non-random patterns, quantified by a low choice entropy relative to a uniform baseline. We position Rushes as a diagnostic benchmark for pluralistic alignment and demonstrate a robust Engagement Gap: state-of-the-art LLMs, including GPT-5, fail to outperform simple baselines. While classical Matrix Factorization (SVD) captures measurable personalized signal (37.7%), frontier LLMs (34.23%) struggle to even match the Popularity Baseline (36.4%) on event-level choice prediction. This gap suggests that single, population-level objectives, like those used in modern RLHF, appear insufficient to capture heterogeneous, context-dependent engagement signals. As a result, even highly capable models default to majority preferences rather than adapting to individual trajectories. We release Rushes to support research into pluralistic alignment and sequential decision-making in generative systems. The full code for the platform and dataset will be available here: https://github.com/microsoft/rushes

</details>


### [138] [Are Diversity Metrics Measuring Diversity? A Capability-Controlled Audit of Majority-Vote Gain in LLM Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20768)
*Donghwan Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过对30个LLM在MMLU-Pro和TruthfulQA上的大量子集进行实证分析，检验了五种多样性度量是否能预测多数投票相对于最佳成员的增益。结果显示：潜在互补性普遍存在但简单多数投票很少超过最强成员；一种联合正确性代理（严格多样性）与1减去平均精度几乎共线性；三种线性列联表统计量在能力控制后只有残差配对共失败关联与增益相关，且方向稳健但幅度依赖配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数投票被广泛认为受益于LLM的多样性，多样性度量被用来选择哪些模型组合。然而，这些多样性度量是真正追踪多样性还是主要反映了模型能力？作者旨在通过严格的能力控制来审计五种多样性度量作为多数投票增益预测因子的有效性。

Method: 作者在31,900个30个LLM的子集（MMLU-Pro上）和29个LLM（TruthfulQA上）上，对五种多样性度量进行审计，作为多数投票相对于最佳成员增益的预测因子，并显式控制能力变量。使用斯皮尔曼相关系数、线性回归等统计方法分析关联的稳定性。

Result: （1）潜在互补性普遍存在（oracle增益在100%子集中为正），但简单多数投票仅在9.98%的典型大小-3子集中击败最强成员；（2）严格多样性与1减去平均精度几乎共线性（rho≈+0.99），原始多样性-增益关联强烈纠缠能力且除一个例外均不稳定；（3）三种列联表统计量代数上不可分离，能力控制后仅剩残差配对共失败关联（更多共享错误对应更低增益），方向稳健但幅度依赖配置；联合原始空间线性回归因构造而秩亏。

Conclusion: 多样性度量在很大程度上是能力代理而非独立多样性指标；多数投票增益主要来源于能力互补而非多样性本身；常见的线性列联表统计量无法分离独立贡献，未来研究需更谨慎地控制能力混淆。

Abstract: Majority voting over LLMs is widely assumed to benefit from diversity, and diversity measures are used to choose which models to combine. We ask whether five such measures track diversity or mainly re-express capability, auditing them as predictors of majority-vote gain over the best member across 31,900 subsets of 30 LLMs on MMLU-Pro (29 on TruthfulQA) under explicit capability controls. Three findings emerge. First, latent complementarity is ubiquitous: oracle gain is positive in 100% of subsets, yet simple voting beats the strongest member in only 9.98% of all canonical size-3 subsets (18.71% with held-out best selection); the pooled size-2-4 rate is 1.27%, partly reflecting deterministic even-size voting behavior. Second, a joint-correctness proxy (strict diversity) is nearly collinear with one minus mean accuracy (size-3 Spearman rho = +0.991 / +0.988); raw diversity-gain associations are strongly capability-entangled and, with one exception, unstable under control. Third, three linear contingency-table statistics are algebraically non-separable; after capability control, the empirically stable remainder is a modest residual pairwise co-failure association in which more shared error corresponds to lower gain. This direction is robust, but its magnitude is configuration-dependent. Joint rawspace linear regressions treating strict diversity, disagreement, and double-fault as independent predictors are rank-deficient by construction.

</details>


### [139] [The Geometry of Personality: Activation Steering with Jungian Cognitive Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20803)
*Liu Zai,Yumeng Wang,Junchen Fu,Joemon M. Jose*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于荣格认知功能的人格表征框架，替代传统大五人格模型的静态特质方法。通过构建包含2100多个角色扮演叙事的评估数据集，在Llama-3.1-8B上验证了激活操控对所有八种荣格认知功能的单调控制，并发现人格信息集中在中间Transformer层、操控向量呈现与理性/非理性功能区分一致的结构化几何关系、以及多维操控方向无法由单功能方向线性组合恢复等关键洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活操控工作主要基于大五人格等静态特质框架来建模人格，但人格可能更适合被表征和操控为一组认知过程。作者希望探索能否用荣格八种认知功能来代表和控制人格，从而提供更可解释、更有效的多维人格控制。

Method: 1) 提出包含荣格评估协议和超过2100个角色扮演叙事的数据集框架；2) 在Llama-3.1-8B模型上进行激活操控向量提取和评估实验；3) 通过操控向量的单调性测试、层分析、几何关系分析以及多维组合实验来验证方法的有效性。

Result: 实验表明：1) 激活操控可对所有八种荣格认知功能实现有效的单调控制；2) 人格信息集中在模型的中间Transformer层；3) 操控向量呈现结构化几何关系，与理性功能（思维、情感）和非理性功能（感觉、直觉）的区分一致；4) 有效的多维操控方向无法由单功能方向的线性组合恢复。

Conclusion: 这些发现为理解LLM激活空间中的人格表征提供了新视角，并建立了一个用于研究可解释、有效且多维人格控制的框架，表明荣格认知功能作为人格操控基础比传统静态特质框架更具优势。

Abstract: Activation steering enables control and interpretation of LLMs, yet existing work primarily models personality through static trait frameworks such as the Big Five. We investigate whether personality can instead be represented and controlled as a set of cognitive processes using the eight Jungian Cognitive Functions. To this end, we introduce a framework comprising a Jungian evaluation protocol and a dataset of over 2,100 role-playing character narrations.
  Activation steering vector extraction and evaluation experiments on Llama-3.1-8B demonstrate effective monotonic control over all eight cognitive functions through activation steering. Beyond controllability, our analysis reveals that: 1. personality information is concentrated in middle transformer layers; 2. steering vectors exhibit structured geometric relationships consistent with distinctions between rational and irrational functions; 3. effective multi-dimensional steering directions cannot be recovered as linear combinations of single-function directions. These findings provide new insights into the representation of personality in LLM activation space and establish a framework for studying interpretable, effective, and multi-dimensional personality control.

</details>


### [140] [REFACT: Adaptive Fact Restatement for Compact and Faithful Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20833)
*Zhensheng Jin,Xin Dai,Zhenghao Liu,Chaojun Xiao,Huiyuan Xie,Yu Gu,Ge Yu,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: REFACT is an adaptive fact-restatement citation framework that trains LLMs to decide when and how to cite contextual evidence during reasoning, using a two-stage SFT-to-RL pipeline with a citation-utility reward to ensure citations are well-formed, traceable, and sufficient for the answer. It improves long-context QA and counterfactual faithfulness while reducing token usage.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs' reasoning traces often drift from context when evidence is sparse, noisy, or conflicting with parametric knowledge. Existing grounding methods (post-hoc citations or in-trace retrieval) do not ensure cited content is sufficient for local inference and final answer, leading to unsupported inferences or indiscriminate fact copying.

Method: REFACT is an adaptive fact-restatement citation framework that trains models to decide when a reasoning step needs grounding and at what granularity source facts should be restated. It uses a two-stage SFT-to-RL pipeline with a citation-utility reward that encourages cited facts to be well-formed, source-traceable, and answer-sufficient.

Result: Experiments on LongBench, LV-Eval, and ConFiQA show REFACT improves long-context QA and counterfactual faithfulness while substantially reducing token consumption. It preserves more answer-bearing evidence with fewer restated facts, yielding denser reasoning traces.

Conclusion: REFACT effectively addresses grounding issues in long-form reasoning by turning citations into answer-supporting intermediate states, achieving better faithfulness and efficiency. Code and data are open-sourced.

Abstract: Large language models increasingly rely on long-form reasoning for complex tasks, yet their reasoning traces may drift away from the supplied context when evidence is sparse, noisy, or in conflict with parametric knowledge. Existing grounding methods either attach citations after generation or encourage evidence retrieval inside the trace, but they often do not ensure that cited content is sufficient for the local inference and final answer. We propose REFACT, an adaptive fact-restatement citation framework that trains models to decide when a reasoning step needs contextual grounding and at what granularity source facts should be restated. This design avoids both unsupported inference and indiscriminate fact copying by turning citations into answer-supporting intermediate states. REFACT is optimized with a two-stage SFT-to-RL pipeline in which a citation-utility reward encourages cited facts to be well-formed, source-traceable, and answer-sufficient. Experiments on LongBench, LV-Eval, and ConFiQA show that REFACT improves long-context QA and counterfactual faithfulness while substantially reducing token consumption. Further analysis shows that REFACT preserves more answer-bearing evidence with fewer restated facts, yielding reasoning traces that are denser rather than longer. All code and data are available at https://github.com/NEUIR/REFACT.

</details>


### [141] [CSPF: A Constrained Shared-Private Fusion Method for Non-Verifiable Preference Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20862)
*Hehao Zhang,Danli Wang,Xinyuan Wang,Xuange Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出约束共享-私有融合（CSPF）方法，将异构的冻结奖励模型作为互补评估器，通过学习融合其隐状态表示来评估非可验证任务，在LM-Arena领域适应和PPE分布外偏好评估上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非可验证任务的可靠评估仍然具有挑战性，现有方法难以充分捕捉人类偏好背后的多样化评估标准。

Method: CSPF将每个专家信号分解为共享表示和专家私有表示，在成对人类偏好监督下学习融合隐状态，促进跨专家对齐同时保留互补视角。

Result: 在LM-Arena目标域适应和PPE分布外偏好评估实验中，CSPF在主要指标上优于单专家奖励模型、标量分数多专家和规则评判基线。

Conclusion: 融合隐状态表示为偏好评估提供了更具表达力的基础，为非可验证偏好任务提供了集成评估信号的实际途径。

Abstract: At present, reliable evaluation of non-verifiable tasks remains challenging. Existing approaches often fail to adequately capture the diverse evaluative criteria underlying human preferences in such tasks. To this end, we propose Constrained Shared-Private Fusion (CSPF), a fusion method that treats heterogeneous frozen reward models as complementary evaluators and learns to integrate their hidden-state representations under pairwise human-preference supervision. CSPF decomposes each expert signal into shared and expert-private representations, encouraging cross-expert alignment while preserving complementary viewpoints. Across experiments on LM-Arena target-domain adaptation and PPE out-of-distribution preference evaluation, CSPF achieves the best performance on the primary metrics among the evaluated single-expert reward-model, scalar-score multi-expert, and rubric-judge baselines. Overall, CSPF suggests that fusing hidden-state representations provides a more expressive basis for preference assessment, offering a practical route toward integrated evaluative signals for non-verifiable preference tasks.

</details>


### [142] [LegalCiteTrust: Benchmarking Citation Trustworthiness in Chinese Long-Form Legal Research Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20872)
*Yunhan Li,Mingjie Xie,Zeyang Shi,Gengshen Wu,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 LegalCiteTrust 基准，用于评估中文长篇幅法律研究报告中引文的可信度，涵盖 72 个密集标注的任务，从覆盖性、支持度和引文可信度三个维度评价报告质量，其中引文可信度通过存在性、忠实性和适用性（E/F/A）操作化。实验表明，检索工具可提升证据支持但不一定能提升可信度分数，而基于 E/F/A 的修订比仅基于存在性的过滤更能改善可信度和最终分数，说明可信的法律研究生成需要检索后对引文进行感知的证据治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长篇幅法律研究报告依赖 LLM 和智能体研究系统，但其可靠性不仅取决于能否回答问题，还取决于所引用法律权威的真实可信程度。即使引用真实来源，报告仍可能遗漏限制条件、错误描述或用于支撑比原文更强的论点，从而带来风险。现有评估缺乏对引文可信度的系统考量，因此需要专门的基准来评估这一维度。

Method: 构建 LegalCiteTrust 基准，包含 72 个密集标注的报告级任务。从三个维度评估：覆盖性（Coverage）、支持度（Support）、引文可信度（Citation Trustworthiness）。引文可信度细化为引文级的存在性（Existence）、忠实性（Fidelity）和适用性（Applicability）。在通用 LLM、深度研究系统和法律专用系统上进行实验，并对比检索工具、基于 E/F/A 的修订与仅存在性过滤的效果。

Result: 通用 LLM、深度研究系统和法律专用系统在任务完成度、证据丰富度、引文密度和引文可靠性上表现出不同行为。检索工具能提升证据支持度，但未必可靠地提升可信度分数。基于 E/F/A 的修订相比仅基于存在性的过滤，能更明显地提升可信度分数和最终分数。

Conclusion: 可信的法律研究生成需要检索后对引文进行感知的证据治理：系统不仅要检索法律权威，还要可靠地选择、描述和应用它们。仅依靠检索工具不足以保证引文可信度，必须进行基于忠实性和适用性的精细化修订。

Abstract: Long-form legal research reports increasingly rely on LLMs and agentic research systems, but their reliability depends not only on answering the task, but also on whether cited legal authorities are trustworthy. A citation can be risky even when it points to a real source: the report may omit limiting conditions, misdescribe the authority, or use it to support a stronger claim than the source allows. We introduce LegalCiteTrust, a benchmark for evaluating citation trustworthiness in Chinese long-form legal research reports. It contains 72 densely annotated report-level tasks and evaluates reports along three dimensions: Coverage, Support, and Citation Trustworthiness. Citation Trustworthiness is operationalized through citation-level Existence, Fidelity, and Applicability (E/F/A). Experiments on general-purpose LLMs, deep-research systems, and legal-specific systems show that task completion, evidence richness, citation density, and citation reliability expose different system behaviors. Retrieval tools can improve evidence support without reliably improving the Trust score, while E/F/A-based revision improves Trust and Final score more clearly than existence-only filtering. These results suggest that trustworthy legal research generation requires citation-aware evidence governance after retrieval: systems must not only retrieve legal authorities, but also select, describe, and apply them reliably.

</details>


### [143] [Tencent WorkBuddy Bench: A Multi-Domain Coding-Agent Benchmark with Contamination-Resistant Task Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20911)
*Tencent WorkBuddy Bench Team,Siqi Cai,Shaopeng Chen,Xiang Fei,Yong Mao,Zihan Xu,Zhiheng Lyu,Zhijian Shao,Yuchen Shi,Shuwen Zhang,Chaofan Qiu,Linjie Che,Xiaoxi Zhao,Feng Wu,Kai Zhang,Chaofan Zhu,Yubin Qi,Xiaoyun Liang,Peijie Dong,Yunhao Zhang,Yuanjie Zhu,Ling Jiang,Xianjun Zhang,Zhehang Chu,Anyuan Sang,Zhen Feng,Sen Nie,Shi Wu,Yuanzhen Xu,Xin Li,Ning Yang,Zhiqiang Dong,Hande Dong,Qiang Lin,Yi Liu,Yunsheng Wu,Ke Li,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Tencent WorkBuddy Bench 是一个面向编码智能体的多领域评估套件，涵盖代码、网页、办公和安全四个工作领域。每个任务通过逆向工程从真实提交/PR/业务场景构建，以短对话角色扮演请求形式呈现，避免可搜索泄露。数据集完全开放（含任务目录、环境镜像、评估框架、测试和参考方案），通过构建方式和版本管理而非保密来抵抗污染。四个子集各有不同的验证方式，在统一协议下运行，但分数不可跨子集比较，无全局平均分。报告了跨模型排行榜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体评估通常局限于单一领域（如代码仓库），且多依赖公开 issue 数据，存在数据泄露和污染风险；缺乏覆盖真实多领域工作场景（如网页开发、办公自动化、安全攻防）的统一评估基准。

Method: 构建了包含四个子集（仓库级工程、前端开发、办公/业务工作流、红/蓝队安全）的评估套件。每个任务从真实 commit/PR/业务场景逆向工程而来，重写为简短口语化角色扮演请求，避免被网络搜索回溯。采用统一的任务目录格式和可复现的评估协议，在 CodeBuddy Code 和 Claude Code 两个智能体框架上运行。数据集开放（含环境镜像、评估框架、测试和参考方案），通过版本管理和构建方式抵抗污染，而非依赖数据保密。

Result: 建立了跨模型排行榜，报告了多个模型家族的表现。每个子集使用不同的评分工具，分数不可跨子集比较，不报告套件全局平均分。完整开放发布使得基准测试可端到端复现和直接审计。

Conclusion: Tencent WorkBuddy Bench 提供了一个多领域、抗污染、完全可复现的编码智能体评估基准，覆盖代码、网页、办公和安全四个真实工作场景，为编码智能体的能力评估提供了更全面和可靠的测试平台。

Abstract: We introduce Tencent WorkBuddy Bench, a multi-domain evaluation suite for coding agents; this report documents its construction methodology, scoring protocol, and a cross-model leaderboard. At its core is a unified evaluation framework for constructing and running distribution-informed coding-agent tasks across four work domains - Code, Web, Office, and Security. Rather than adapting public issue text, every task is reverse-engineered from a real commit, pull request, or business scenario and rewritten as a short, colloquial, role-played request, so that a task's prompt is not recoverable by web-searching the underlying issue, pull request, or commit thread. Because the dataset is released openly - task directories, environment images, evaluation harness, tests, and reference solutions - contamination resistance rests on this construction together with dataset versioning rather than on secrecy. The four subsets - repository-level engineering, front-end development, office and business workflows, and red-/blue-team security - probe complementary facets of real work, each with its own verification style. All are packaged in a uniform task-directory format and run, under a uniform and reproducible protocol, on two agent harnesses (CodeBuddy Code and Claude Code); the full open release makes the benchmark reproducible end to end and directly auditable, since any third party can re-run each task and inspect its content. Because each subset uses a different scoring instrument, scores are not comparable across subsets and the suite reports no suite-wide average. We report a cross-model leaderboard across several model families.

</details>


### [144] [From a Word-Level Dictionary to Sentence-Level Semantics: Multilingual Grievance Labelling with Contextual Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20946)
*Lin Tian,Marian-Andrei Rizoiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在线文本中 grievance（不满/怨愤）的测量方法。传统的词级词典（如 Grievance Dictionary）通过匹配加权术语来评分，但无法区分术语是陈述、引用、否定还是谴责，且评估存在循环论证问题（词典本身将评测池分为正负例，导致 AUROC 虚高）。作者保留了词典的 22 构念本体，但用上下文阅读模型替代词匹配，并在非循环基准（包含无条件随机、词典正例、词典负例三层）上评估。结果表明，阅读完整帖子而非仅目标句子，在词典沉默的区域帮助最大，将词典负例文本的平均精度从 0.14 提升到 0.20，尤其在引用、隐含和跨句怨愤上增益显著。结论是：通过阅读上下文可以更忠实地测量 grievance，在词典未选择的文本上测试也更诚实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的词级词典（如 Grievance Dictionary）在测量在线文本中的 grievance 时存在局限：无法解析术语的语境（是陈述、引用、否定还是谴责），且评估池被词典本身的选择规则污染，导致性能评分虚高。因此需要更准确的测量方法。

Method: 保留词典的 22 构念本体，但用上下文阅读模型（context-reading models）替代词匹配。构建非循环基准，将文本分为无条件随机、词典正例、词典负例三层，涵盖五种语言。比较仅阅读目标句子与阅读完整帖子的效果。

Result: 原词典的宏 AUROC 0.686 实际上是构造决定的 0.500 基线。上下文阅读模型在词典负例文本上的平均精度从 0.14 提升到 0.20，尤其在引用、隐含和跨句怨愤上增益最大。

Conclusion: 阅读上下文可以更忠实地测量 grievance，在词典未选择的文本上测试也更加诚实。发布代码和基准。

Abstract: Grievance is one of the warning signs analysts look for when assessing threats of violence. It is increasingly measured at scale from online text, most often with word-level lexicons like the Grievance Dictionary that score by matching weighted terms. Such matching is a fast and transparent proxy, but it cannot resolve whether a term is asserted, quoted, negated, or condemned. These lexicons are also often evaluated on pools enriched with the very examples they retrieve, so a high score partly reflects agreement with the lexicon's own selection rule. Examining a five-language, 2{,}000-item evaluation pool, we find its halves separated almost perfectly by the lexicon itself: every item labeled ``random'' is in fact lexicon-negative, so the lexicon's apparent macro-AUROC of 0.686 collapses to a 0.500 floor fixed by construction. We keep the dictionary's 22-construct ontology but replace term matching with context-reading models, evaluated on a non-circular benchmark that separates unconditional-random, lexicon-positive, and lexicon-negative strata across five languages. Reading the full post rather than the target sentence alone helps most where the lexicon is silent, raising average precision on lexicon-negative text from 0.14 to 0.20, with the largest gains on quoted, implicit, and cross-sentence grievance. Together, these results show that grievance is measured more faithfully by reading the surrounding context, and more honestly when tested on text the lexicon did not select. We release our code and benchmark at https://github.com/behavioral-ds/multilingual_grievance.

</details>


### [145] [Where Animacy Lives in Large Language Models: Tracing the Circuits of the Animacy Concept](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20995)
*Samuele Punzo,Giovanni Cinà,Sandro Pezzelle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探究大型语言模型（LLMs）是否具有处理有生性（animate vs. inanimate）概念的因果机制。通过构建最小对数据集并对四个开源模型进行电路发现，作者发现了一个负责处理有生性的电路，但该电路定位不如其他已知电路集中，且在模型间和任务间仅部分泛化，证实了有生性概念的分布性、上下文依赖性和渐进性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLMs能够区分有生与无生概念，但尚不清楚这种能力是否源于模型内部局部的因果机制。作者希望探究这一行为是否可以追溯至一组局部相关的组件和连接，即是否存在“有生性电路”。

Method: 作者构建了一个受控的最小对数据集，对四个开放权重模型（如LLaMA等）进行电路发现（circuit discovery），通过深入实验和消融研究，定位并分析负责有生性处理的因果机制。

Result: 成功发现了一个负责处理有生性的因果电路，但该电路定位不如其他已知电路（如间接宾语识别电路）集中；其泛化能力有限，在不同模型和不同有生性任务间仅部分通用。

Conclusion: 有生性概念在LLMs中是通过分布式、上下文依赖且具有渐进性的方式处理的，而非高度局部的模块化机制。这为理解语言模型中抽象概念的表征提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Distinguishing animate from inanimate concepts in written language requires more than shallow text processing, as it involves recognizing complex selectional constraints and contextual cues, such as verb-argument interactions. Yet, current large language models (LLMs) appear to be capable of doing it. We investigate whether this animacy-sensitive behavior of LLMs can be traced to a localized set of causally relevant components and connections. To do so, we construct a controlled dataset of minimal pairs and perform circuit discovery on four open-weight models. Through in-depth experiments and ablations, we show that a causal mechanism responsible for handling animacy in these models does exist, thus discovering an animacy circuit. At the same time, this circuit appears to be less localized compared to other known ones and generalizes only partially across models and animacy tasks, confirming the distributed, context-dependent, and somewhat graded nature of the animacy concept.

</details>


### [146] [CultureTalk-ID: A Multi-Task Dialogue Benchmark for Cultural Commonsense in Indonesian Local Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21016)
*Muhammad Dehan Al Kautsar,Salsabila Pranida,Bilal Elbouardi,Fajri Koto*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 CultureTalk-ID，首个基于对话的印度尼西亚文化常识基准，涵盖 11 种语言、13 个文化主题的 4,496 个对话，并包含三项互补任务（对话式多项选择推理、文化忠实机器翻译、语言引导），以评估 LLM 在对话语境中理解、迁移和生成文化语言的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的印尼文化常识基准仅使用简短、孤立的提示来评估 LLM，剥离了文化细微差别实际出现的对话语境，无法真实反映模型在对话中理解和运用文化常识的能力。

Method: 通过多阶段人工流程，由母语者策划了 4,496 个文化对话，涵盖 11 种语言（印尼语及其地方语言）和 13 个文化显著主题，并设计了三项任务：基于对话的多项选择文化常识推理、文化忠实机器翻译、语言引导，分别评估模型对文化语言的理解、迁移和生成能力。

Result: 构建了一个高质量、真实的对话式文化常识基准，提供了多任务评估框架，用于检验 LLM 在对话语境中的文化常识表现。

Conclusion: CultureTalk-ID 填补了现有基准缺乏对话语境的空白，为评估 LLM 在印尼多元文化背景下的文化能力提供了更全面、更真实的测试平台。

Abstract: Culture is lived through conversation, yet existing Indonesian cultural commonsense benchmarks evaluate LLMs on short and isolated prompts, stripping away the dialogic context in which cultural nuances actually surface. We introduce CultureTalk-ID, the first dialogue-based benchmark for cultural commonsense in Indonesian and its local languages, comprising 4,496 culturally grounded dialogues across 11 languages and 13 culturally salient topics, curated through a multi-stage human pipeline with native speakers to ensure authenticity. CultureTalk-ID introduces three complementary tasks, namely dialogue-based multiple-choice cultural commonsense reasoning, culturally faithful machine translation, and language steering, which jointly probe whether LLMs can understand, transfer, and generate culturally grounded language.

</details>


### [147] [Sample-Efficient Learning from Agent Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21051)
*Chenhui Gou,Haoqin Tu,Yunhao Fang,Jianfei Cai,Hamid Rezatofighi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出经验蒸馏（Experience Distillation）方法，将智能体通过上下文学习从交互历史中获得的经验内化到模型权重中，无需额外环境交互，在软件工程任务和文本冒险游戏中保留至少64.8%的上下文学习收益，而直接微调仅保留3.8%，且与经典强化学习基线相比样本效率提高至少9.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的智能体学习常受限于昂贵的环境交互成本（如耗时实验或人工反馈）。上下文学习虽能从交互历史中高效学习，但一旦经验移出上下文，增益即消失。上下文蒸馏虽能内化上下文信息，但如何将其应用于智能体的交互历史而不牺牲环境样本效率仍未充分探索。

Method: 将问题定义为“经验蒸馏”（Experience Distillation），开发了一种无需在收集的经验之外进行额外环境交互的实现。该方法基于上下文蒸馏，将智能体通过上下文学习从试错交互历史中获得的知识转移到模型权重中。

Result: 在749个软件工程任务和6个文本冒险游戏上，经验蒸馏保留了至少64.8%的上下文学习收益，而直接监督微调仅恢复3.8%。与经典强化学习基线相比，上下文学习结合经验蒸馏后，在匹配性能的同时减少了至少9.6倍的环境样本需求。

Conclusion: 经验蒸馏能有效将上下文学习获得的经验内化为模型参数，避免上下文窗口限制，显著提升样本效率，为实际环境交互成本高的智能体学习提供了一种实用方法。

Abstract: Real-world agent learning is often constrained by costly environment interactions, such as running time-consuming experiments or obtaining human feedback. In-context learning offers a highly sample-efficient way for agents to learn from their own interaction histories, but its gains disappear once that experience is removed from the context. Separately, context distillation provides a mechanism for internalizing contextual information into model weights. However, applying it to agents' interaction histories without sacrificing environment sample efficiency remains underexplored. We term this problem Experience Distillation and develop an implementation that requires no further environment interaction beyond the collected experience. Experiments on 749 curated software-engineering tasks and six text-adventure games show that it retains at least 64.8\% of the gains from in-context learning across both domains, whereas direct supervised fine-tuning on the collected experience recovers only 3.8\%. Compared with classical reinforcement-learning baselines, in-context learning from trial-and-error experience followed by Experience Distillation matches their performance with at least \(9.6\times\) fewer environment samples.

</details>


### [148] [QuantiBias: Benchmarking Quantization-Induced Bias in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21063)
*Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，对大型语言模型进行量化（从全精度压缩为低精度）的主要副作用是增加了模型在开放式生成中的偏见，而这种偏见不会被标准安全检查（如拒绝有害请求、不拒绝良性提示、选择题公平性等）所检测到。作者提出了QuantiBias基准测试，通过生成式多语言刻板印象探测、拒绝控制、多项选择控制来隔离开放式生成中的偏见，并评估内容严重程度。实验表明，在不同模型族和量化方法中，量化会分配额外精度给缺乏偏见预防信号的能力数据；思维链推理在某些模型族中能将偏见减半，但对其他模型族无效。结论是量化后的模型必须针对开放式偏见重新评估，而不仅仅依赖其已通过的短格式安全检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化被广泛用于压缩LLM以提高效率，但人们默认认为量化是无害的，很少重新检查其安全性。作者发现量化的主要副作用是增加偏见，而标准安全评估（如拒绝有害请求、避免过度拒绝、选择题公平性）无法检测到这种偏见。现有基准测试忽略了量化对开放式生成偏见的影响，需要一种专门针对量化模型开放式偏见的新评估方法。

Method: 作者提出了QuantiBias基准测试，包含三个方面：（1）生成式多语言刻板印象探测（在8种语言中探测），配合独立的偏见评判器；（2）拒绝控制和多项选择控制，以隔离开放式生成中的偏见；（3）评估生成内容的严重程度。实验在两个骨干模型（Qwen和Gemma）上进行，涵盖了5个模型族和8个基准测试，对比了有无思维链推理的情况。

Result: 量化模型在所有标准安全检查（拒绝有害请求、不拒绝良性提示、选择题公平性）中都表现正常，但在开放式问答中，大约每四个回答中就有一个（约24%-27%）表现出刻板印象。量化对不同模型族的影响不同：部分模型族中推理能减半偏见，其他模型族则无效。量化方法将额外精度分配给缺乏偏见预防信号的能力数据。

Conclusion: 量化的主要副作用是增加开放式生成中的偏见，而标准安全评估无法捕捉到这一点。量化后的模型必须针对开放式偏见进行重新评估，不能仅依赖其已通过的短格式安全检查。作者提供了QuantiBias基准测试作为解决方案，并建议在量化过程中纳入偏见预防信号。

Abstract: Almost every large language model that reaches a broad audience is quantized: trained in full precision, then compressed for efficiency. This step is assumed harmless and its safety is rarely re-checked. We find its principal side effect is increased bias that standard safety evaluation misses. Holding the model, its training, and the prompts fixed, a quantized model still refuses harmful requests, still avoids over-refusing benign prompts, and still selects the unbiased multiple-choice answer. Yet asked an open-ended question, the same model volunteers stereotypes in all eight languages we probe, in roughly one in four open-ended answers under an independent judge (~24% to ~27% across the compression ladder): it passes every standard check and still reaches users measurably more biased. The selective gap is a robust finding; whether open-ended bias further increases with compression is less certain, sensitive to the judge that scores it. We address both with \textbf{QuantiBias}, a benchmark that pairs a generative, multilingual stereotype probe with the refusal and multiple-choice controls that isolate open-ended generation, contrasts each build with and without reasoning, and rates the content severity of what it generates. Across two backbone models (Qwen and Gemma), a five-family screen, and eight benchmarks, quantizers allocate their extra precision by capability data that carries no bias-prevention signal, and reasoning before answering roughly halves the effect on some families while doing nothing on others. A quantized build must be re-evaluated for open-ended bias, not only on the short-form safeguards it already passes.

</details>


### [149] [PrefReward: Learning User Preference Matrix for Personalized Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21067)
*Yue Wu,Chengbing Wang,Yimeng Bai,Xiaoyan Zhao,Yang Zhang,Fuli Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PrefReward 是一个通过显式偏好矩阵和 KL 散度奖励信号引导 LLM 生成个性化内容的框架，在 LongLaMP 数据集上优于非个性化和检索基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 个性化方法依赖模型参数中的隐式表示，难以解释用户偏好或处理长上下文依赖。

Method: 两阶段框架：首先提取用户特定的偏好矩阵以概括个体风格倾向，然后通过 KL 散度奖励函数将该矩阵融入解码过程以指导生成。

Result: 在 LongLaMP 数据集上的实验表明，PrefReward 在生成质量和个性化可解释性方面均优于非个性化和基于检索的基线。

Conclusion: PrefReward 通过显式建模用户风格偏好并作为奖励信号集成，有效提升了 LLM 个性化生成的可解释性和长上下文处理能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable ability in generating personalized content by leveraging user histories and contextual cues. However, most existing personalization approaches rely on implicit representations within model parameters, making it difficult to interpret user-specific preferences or effectively handle long-context dependencies. To address these challenges, we propose PrefReward, a novel preference-aware generative framework that explicitly models user styles through a structured preference matrix and integrates it into the decoding process as a reward signal. PrefReward consists of two stages: (1) extracting a user-specific preference matrix that summarizes individual stylistic tendencies, and (2) using the matrix to guide generation via a KL-divergence-based reward function. Experiments on the LongLaMP dataset show that PrefReward outperforms non-personalized and retrieval-based baselines in both generation quality and personalization interpretability.

</details>


### [150] [One More Turn, Less Regret: A Regret-Based Multi-Turn Benchmark for LLMs' Clarification Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21143)
*Minh Ngoc Ta,My Anh Tran Nguyen,Duong D. Nguyen,Yuxia Wang,Preslav Nakov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RegretBench is a multi-turn benchmark that evaluates clarification as policy behavior in conversational LLM assistants, using a hidden-intent formulation, semantic-state tracking, and a regret-based objective to measure value loss relative to a reference policy. Experiments show that final success alone is insufficient; models must ask the right question at the right time and stop once the user's intent is clear.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ambiguous user requests make clarification a sequential decision problem for conversational LLM assistants; existing evaluations focus on isolated question quality rather than overall clarification policy behavior.

Method: RegretBench provides a hidden-intent formulation of ambiguity, supports free-form interaction grounded in semantic-state tracking, and introduces a regret-based objective that measures how much value a model loses relative to a reference clarification policy. It jointly measures intent resolution, interaction cost, ineffective clarification, and regret.

Result: Experiments on open-domain QA and product recommendation show that models with similar accuracy can differ substantially in efficiency, robustness to user behaviors, and stopping decisions; final success alone is insufficient.

Conclusion: Effective clarification requires more than plausible questions: models must ask the right question at the right time and stop once the user's intended meaning is clear.

Abstract: Ambiguous user requests make clarification a sequential decision problem for conversational LLM assistants: they must decide whether to ask, what to ask, when to stop, and when to answer. We introduce RegretBench, a multi-turn benchmark that evaluates clarification as policy behavior rather than isolated question quality. RegretBench provides a hidden-intent formulation of ambiguity, supports free-form interaction grounded in semantic-state tracking, and introduces a regret-based objective that measures how much value a model loses relative to a reference clarification policy. Experiments on open-domain QA and product recommendation scenarios show that final success alone is insufficient, as models with similar accuracy can differ substantially in efficiency, robustness to user behaviors, and stopping decisions. By jointly measuring intent resolution, interaction cost, ineffective clarification, and regret, RegretBench reveals whether models clarify usefully and efficiently. Our results show that effective clarification requires more than plausible questions: models must ask the right question at the right time and stop once the user's intended meaning is clear.

</details>


### [151] [Progressive Cramming: Reliable Token Compression and What It Reveals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21231)
*Dmitrii Tarasov,Timofei Lashukov,Elizaveta Goncharova,Andrey Kuznetsov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Progressive cramming grows token sequences into learned embeddings token-by-token until reconstruction fails within a fixed optimization budget. It reveals that perfect reconstruction is possible but brittle, not preserving semantic meaning, causing accuracy drops on downstream tasks and capability collapse under generative evaluation. This shows perfect reconstruction is insufficient for meaningful compression.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To distinguish whether residual errors in token cramming (compressing sequences into embeddings with near-perfect reconstruction) arise from optimization failures or fundamental limits, and to study the sufficiency of perfect reconstruction for meaningful compression.

Method: Introduces progressive cramming: grows the target prefix token-by-token, stopping when reconstruction is no longer achievable within a fixed optimization budget. Analyzes trajectories in embedding space, prepends crammed embeddings to models for downstream evaluation, and employs causal attention-knockout interventions to trace degradation.

Result: Progressive trajectories occupy low-dimensional structure. Prepending a crammed embedding causes moderate but consistent accuracy drop on multiple-choice benchmarks even with original prefix in context, and collapses capability under generative evaluation. Attention-knockout interventions attribute degradation to early-layer interactions between the embedding and the model.

Conclusion: Perfect reconstruction, achievable through brittle steering rather than transferable semantics, is insufficient for meaningful compression. Progressive cramming serves as a tool for studying compression limits.

Abstract: Token cramming compresses sequences into learned embeddings with near-perfect reconstruction, but fixed token budgets and 99\% accuracy thresholds leave it unclear whether residual errors reflect optimization failures or fundamental limits. We introduce progressive cramming, which grows the target prefix token-by-token, stopping only when reconstruction is no longer achievable within a fixed optimization budget. Progressive trajectories occupy low-dimensional structure in embedding space. Prepending a crammed embedding causes a moderate but consistent accuracy drop on multiple-choice benchmarks even with the original prefix in context, and collapses capability almost entirely under generative evaluation. Causal attention-knockout interventions trace this degradation to the embedding's interactions in the model's early layers. These results position progressive cramming as a tool for studying compression limits and show that perfect reconstruction - achievable through brittle steering rather than transferable semantics - is insufficient for meaningful compression.

</details>


### [152] [slang.gr as a Large-Scale Crowdsourced Resource for Non-Standard Greek](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21255)
*Panagiotis Papadakos,Katerina Papantoniou,Dimitris Plexousakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模计算研究了希腊非标准语言众包词典slang.gr，通过将噪声标签映射到结构化多层分类体系，分析了希腊俚语的语言结构及其贡献者社区行为，并提出了基于社区的信心评分方法，为计算社会语言学和NLP研究奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 俚语是日常语言的核心组成部分，反映语言创造力、社会身份和文化变迁，但其动态和非标准特性使其难以计算建模。现有研究缺乏对非标准语言的大规模系统分析，尤其是希腊语等低资源语言。

Method: 将slang.gr众包词典中的噪声大众分类标签映射到结构化多层分类体系（涵盖语义类别和社会语言学元数据），利用该表示分析希腊俚语的语言结构及贡献者社区行为。基于用户角色、交互模式和审核信号提出社区驱动的信心评分。

Result: 俚语高度集中于与人物相关和评价性语言，表现出高度的形态创造力；贡献者社区呈现高度倾斜的参与模式、短用户生命周期和重叠社区结构；基于分类体系的表示提高了可解释性，同时保留了行为结构的关键特征。

Conclusion: 该工作将slang.gr确立为非标准希腊语的计算资源，为社会语言学NLP、偏见分析以及大型语言模型中非正式语言研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Slang is a central component of everyday language, reflecting linguistic creativity, social identity, and cultural change, yet its dy- namic and non-standard nature makes it difficult to model computationally. We present the first large-scale computational study of slang.gr, a crowdsourced lexicon of Greek non-standard language, combining lexical content, user-generated tags, and interaction data. To enable the systematic analysis, we map noisy folksonomic tags to a structured multi-layer taxonomy capturing both semantic categories and sociolinguistic metadata. Using this representation, we analyze the linguistic structure of Greek slang and the behavior of its contributor community. We find that slang is strongly centered on person-related and evaluative language, exhibits high morphological creativity, and is shaped by highly skewed participation with short user lifespans and overlapping communities. Building on these signals, we introduce a community-based confidence score for definitions that integrates user roles, interaction patterns, and moderation signals. Our results show that taxonomy-based representations improve interpretability while retaining meaningful aspects of behavioral structure, enabling a more structured and interpretable analysis of confidence signals. Overall, this work establishes slang.gr as a computational resource for non-standard Greek and provides a foundation for sociolinguistic NLP, bias analysis, and the study of informal language in LLMs.

</details>


### [153] [A Comparative Evaluation of Embeddings and LLMs in a Greek Book Publisher Setting - The CUP Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21274)
*Katerina Papantoniou,Panagiotis Papadakos,Theodore Patkos,Dimitris Garefalakis,Nikos Vardakis,Dimitris Plexousakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了CUP，一个希腊语图书检索基准数据集，包含868条目录记录和104个专家标注查询，并评估了稀疏、稠密、混合及LLM辅助检索方法。实验表明多语言嵌入优于希腊语专用模型，混合检索整体表现最佳；BM25擅长命名实体查询，而稠密和混合方法在自然语言、噪声、跨语言和概念查询上更优；LLM的目录摘要和事后过滤能提升特定场景的检索效果但成本较高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏面向希腊语的图书检索基准数据集，无法系统评估不同检索方法在希腊语场景下的表现，特别是应对词汇、语义、噪声和跨语言查询的能力。

Method: 构建了包含868条目录记录和104个专家标注查询的CUP基准数据集，采用分级相关性判断。对比了稀疏检索(BM25)、稠密检索(sentence-transformers)、混合检索以及LLM辅助检索方法，并进行了查询级别的细粒度分析，考察了字段感知提示、目录摘要及LLM事后过滤等策略。

Result: 多语言嵌入优于希腊语专用模型；混合检索整体最佳；BM25在命名实体查询上最优，稠密和混合方法在自然语言、噪声、跨语言和概念查询上更优；字段感知提示影响因模型而异；LLM目录摘要提升目录检索性能；LLM事后过滤提升早期检索但成本高昂。

Conclusion: CUP为希腊语检索提供了真实场景下的评估基准，覆盖词汇、语义、噪声和跨语言等多种查询类型，为未来希腊语信息检索研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present CUP, a Greek book retrieval benchmark consisting of 868 catalog records and 104 expert-annotated queries with graded relevance judgments. We evaluate sparse (BM25), dense (sentence-transformers), hybrid, and LLM-assisted retrieval methods in this book-search setting. Multilingual embeddings outperform Greek-specific models, while hybrid retrieval performs best overall. A query-level analysis shows that BM25 excels at named-entity queries, while dense and hybrid methods improve natural-language, noisy, cross-lingual, and concept queries. Field-aware prompting has model-specific effects, while LLM TOC summarization improves TOC-only retrieval and LLM post-filtering improves early-stage retrieval at a high cost. Overall, CUP enables real-world evaluation of Greek retrieval across lexical, semantic, noisy, and cross-lingual queries.

</details>


### [154] [A Unified Moral-Value Dataset for Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21279)
*Zhaohui Zeng,Florian Mai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个统一的道德价值数据集，用于指令微调，旨在解决大语言模型的价值对齐问题。通过合并现有道德价值数据集并转换为指令-响应格式，作者展示了在混合通用任务数据集和该数据集上进行训练可以保持通用任务性能，并初步观察了混合比例对价值导向任务性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型发展迅速，但如何将其与特定人类价值观对齐仍是一个开放问题。现有指令微调数据集未专门针对道德场景和行为设计，因此需要构建一个可直接用于指令微调的统一道德价值数据集。

Method: 作者将现有的道德价值数据集合并为一个统一语料库，并将其转换为指令-响应格式，从而构建了一个可直接用于指令微调的道德价值数据集。他们使用混合数据集（通用任务数据集+该道德价值数据集）进行训练。

Result: 实验表明，在通用任务数据集混合该道德价值数据集进行训练，可以保持通用任务性能；同时初步观察到混合比例会影响价值导向任务的表现。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个用于指令微调的道德价值数据集，为后续的对齐研究提供了有用资源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have developed rapidly and become valuable tools in everyday life. However, how to align LLMs to a particular set of human values is still an open problem. Recent studies show that instruction tuning has strong potential for zero-shot tasks and may serve as an effective approach to addressing value alignment. Nevertheless, although many datasets for instruction tuning already exist, they are not specifically designed around moral scenarios and behaviors. We construct a unified moral-value dataset that can be directly used for instruction tuning. This dataset is built upon existing moral-value datasets by merging them into a unified corpus and converting them into an instruction-response format. We show that training on a mixed dataset combining general task datasets with our dataset preserves general-task performance, and we report preliminary observations on how the mixing ratio affects value-oriented task performance. Our work provides a moral-value dataset for instruction tuning and offers a useful resource for further alignment research. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/teohzzh/value-for-instruction-tuning.

</details>


### [155] [news-crawler-LM: A Small Long-Context Model For High-Quality News Crawling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21284)
*Pascal Stolzenburg,Jonas Golde,Max Dallabetta,Alan Akbik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 news-crawler-LM，一个经过高质量人工验证数据微调的小型长上下文语言模型，用于将新闻网页的原始 HTML 转换为纯文本和结构化 JSON（包含标题、作者、发布日期、正文等字段）。实验表明，该模型在 HTML-to-Markdown 和 HTML-to-JSON 任务上显著优于基线，但在纯文本提取任务上仅略优于规则解析库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新闻网页的 HTML 布局异构、标记不一致且包含大量样板内容（导航、广告），规则爬虫需手工配置才能泛化，大语言模型虽灵活但计算成本过高，难以实际部署。因此需要一个兼顾准确性与效率的轻量级解决方案。

Method: 基于 Fundus 新闻爬取库中高质量、人工验证的数据，微调一个小型长上下文语言模型（news-crawler-LM），使其能够将原始 HTML 直接转换为纯文本或结构化的 JSON 格式。

Result: 在 HTML-to-Markdown 任务上 BLEU 提升 +4.8、METEOR 提升 +6.1；在 HTML-to-JSON 任务上 BLEU 提升 +2.2、METEOR 提升 +4.1。但在对未见出版商的 HTML-to-plaintext 评估中，模型仅略优于其他规则解析库。

Conclusion: news-crawler-LM 在结构化提取任务上表现出色，但在纯文本提取方面优势有限。模型及所有工件均已开源，供研究社区使用。

Abstract: Extracting structured content from news pages remains challenging due to heterogeneous HTML layouts, inconsistent markup, and substantial boilerplate such as navigation elements and advertisements. Rule-based news crawlers can achieve high extraction accuracy by encoding site-specific structure, but require manual configuration in order to generalize to new publishers. Large language models provide a more flexible alternative by reducing the need for handcrafted rules, but their high computational cost limits practical deployment. In this paper, we introduce news-crawler-LM, a small long-context language model fine-tuned on high-quality, human-validated extractions from the Fundus news-crawling library. Our model converts raw HTML into plaintext and structured JSON, including fields such as headline, author, publication date, and article body. In our experiments, news-crawler-LM outperforms strong baselines in HTML-to-Markdown and HTML-to-JSON extraction, improving performance by +4.8 BLEU and +6.1 METEOR in the HTML-to-Markdown task, and by +2.2 BLEU and +4.1 METEOR in the HTML-to-JSON task. However, we also observe that our model only slightly better compared to other rule-based parsing libraries on the HTML-to-plaintext task in evaluations on previously unseen publishers. We release all models and artifacts to the research community.

</details>


### [156] [Adaptive Depth Sparse Framework: Similarity-Driven Resource Allocation for Pre-Trained LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21291)
*Yidu Wu,Xiang Wang,Kejie Zhao,Zhangchi Wang,Qinghai Guo,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种无需全量重训练的深度稀疏化框架 AdaDSF，基于层间余弦相似度统计分配每层 token 保留率，并通过轻量路由器选择重要 token 及特征保持对齐目标，在 GPT-NeoX 和 Qwen2.5 上显著降低推理 FLOPs，性能接近稠密模型，且在同等稀疏度下精度损失小于 MoD、D-LLM、DLO 等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 推理加速方法通常需要任务特定的微调或从头训练，适应性成本高且跨任务泛化受限；同时 Transformer 架构推理开销大，需要一种无需全量重训练即可将预训练 LLM 转换为深度稀疏模型的方法。

Method: 提出 AdaDSF 框架，核心包括：1) 利用层输入与输出隐藏状态间的余弦相似度统计刻画各层对表示变换的不等贡献；2) 根据相似度统计为每层分配 token 保留比率；3) 训练轻量路由器在每个层选择信息量大的 token；4) 引入特征保持对齐目标，使稀疏模型与稠密模型的中间及最终表示相匹配。

Result: 在 GPT-NeoX 和 Qwen2.5 的语言建模与常识推理任务上，AdaDSF 大幅减少推理 FLOPs，同时保持与稠密模型接近的性能；在同等稀疏度下，其精度退化一致地小于 MoD、D-LLM 和 DLO 等强基线。

Conclusion: AdaDSF 提供了一种高效、无需全重训练的 LLM 深度稀疏化方案，通过层间相似性引导的 token 选择与特征对齐，在推理加速与性能保持之间取得了优异平衡，且优于现有稀疏化方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong generation and reasoning performance, but the Transformer architecture incurs high inference cost. Existing acceleration methods often rely on task-specific fine-tuning or training from scratch, increasing adaptation cost and limiting cross-task usability. We present an Adaptive Depth Sparse Framework (AdaDSF) that converts off-the-shelf pre-trained LLMs into depth-sparse models without full retraining. Our key insight is that layers contribute unequally to representation transformation, characterized by the cosine similarity between layer input and output hidden states. Based on this, AdaDSF assigns layer-wise token retention ratios from similarity statistics, uses a lightweight router to select informative tokens at each layer, and introduces a feature-preserving alignment objective to match intermediate and final representations between sparse and dense models. On GPT-NeoX and Qwen2.5 over language modeling and commonsense reasoning, AdaDSF substantially reduces inference FLOPs while preserving performance close to dense counterparts. Under comparable sparsity, AdaDSF consistently yields smaller accuracy degradation than strong baselines including MoD, D-LLM, and DLO.

</details>


### [157] [GRADRAG: Cross-Component Prompt Adaptation for Coordinated Multi-Agent RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21324)
*Paolo Pedinotti,Enrico Santus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GRADRAG是一个跨组件提示自适应框架，将RAG流水线建模为计算图，通过传播结构化评估反馈来更新上游智能体（检索器、图构建器、回答器等）。评估器评判下游答案和支撑证据，生成可操作的反馈，提示优化器据此迭代更新自适应智能体，并在输出满意时触发早停。在SQUALITY和QMSUM基准上，GRADRAG在两种检索范式下均优于仅更新最终生成器的单步精化基线，获得12-15个百分点的净偏好优势，且大多数收益在两次迭代内实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统多采用多个LLM智能体，但大多数先前工作孤立地优化各组件，而非协调整个流水线的改进。缺乏跨组件的协同优化机制。

Method: GRADRAG框架：将RAG流水线建模为计算图，包含评估器（Evaluator）评判下游答案和支撑证据并生成可操作反馈，提示优化器（Prompt Optimizer）据此迭代更新自适应智能体（检索器、图构造器、回答器等），评估器还能在输出满意时触发早停。

Result: 在SQUALITY和QMSUM基准上，采用两种检索范式（扁平块检索+IRCoT风格查询精化、基于图的检索+实体关系图构建与迭代丰富），GRADRAG均优于仅更新最终生成器的单步精化基线，LLM评判的成对比较中获得12-15个百分点的净偏好优势，大多数收益在两次迭代内实现。

Conclusion: GRADRAG通过跨组件提示自适应和结构化反馈传播，有效协调RAG流水线中各智能体的改进，显著优于仅优化下游生成器的传统方法，且收敛速度快。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems increasingly employ multiple LLM agents. Yet, most prior work optimizes components in isolation rather than coordinating improvements across the pipeline. We introduce GRADRAG, a framework for cross-component prompt adaptation that models the RAG pipeline as a computational graph and propagates structured evaluation feedback to update upstream agents. An Evaluator critiques downstream answers and supporting evidence, producing actionable feedback that a Prompt Optimizer uses to iteratively update adaptive agents, such as retrievers, graph constructors, and answerers. The Evaluator also triggers early stopping when the output is deemed satisfactory. We evaluate GRADRAG on the SQUALITY and QMSUM benchmarks under two retrieval paradigms: flat chunk-based retrieval using IRCoT-style query refinement (Trivedi et al., 2023), and graph-based retrieval that constructs and iteratively enriches an entity-relation graph from the document. Across both settings, GRADRAG consistently outperforms one-step refinement baselines that update only the final generator, achieving a 12-15 percentage point net preference margin in LLM-judged pairwise comparisons, with most gains realized within two refinement iterations.

</details>


### [158] [Phonetic forced alignment for low-resource language varieties: Model training and evaluation on Chengdu Mandarin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21332)
*Zhiheng Qian,Aini Li,Hai Hu,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过训练文本相关（GMM-HMM）和文本无关（微调预训练音频编码器）的对齐模型，为低资源语言变体成都话建立了实用的引导式对齐流水线，在专家标注测试集上分别将音素边界差异降低31.8%和61.2%，显著优于标准普通话基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音对齐系统缺乏针对低资源语言变体的专门模型，而成都话等方言变体缺少大规模的标注数据与专用对齐工具，导致语音学研究受限。

Method: 基于17小时成都话语料库和自建G2P词典，训练了文本相关的GMM-HMM模型（Chengdu-MFA）；同时利用Chengdu-MFA的伪标签微调预训练音频编码器进行帧分类，实现文本无关对齐（Chengdu-FC）。

Result: 在专家标注测试集上，Chengdu-MFA将平均音素边界差异降低31.8%，Chengdu-FC降低61.2%，均显著优于标准普通话基线。

Conclusion: 该工作为低资源语言变体建立了一种无需人工标注、节约时间与精力的实用引导式对齐流水线，能够快速开发精准的对齐模型。

Abstract: Phonetic forced alignment is a key technique in phonetic research, yet existing alignment systems lack specialized models for low-resource language varieties. We address this by training text-dependent and text-independent aligners for Chengdu Mandarin using a 17-hour corpus and a custom G2P dictionary. We trained a text-dependent GMM-HMM model (Chengdu-MFA) and fine-tuned a pretrained audio encoder on frame classification with Chengdu-MFA's pseudo label for text-independent alignment (Chengdu-FC). Evaluation on an expert-annotated test set show that both methods significantly outperform Standard Mandarin baselines. Chengdu-MFA reduced average phone boundary differences by 31.8%, while Chengdu-FC achieved a 61.2% reduction. This work establishes a practical bootstrapping pipeline for developing accurate aligners for under-resourced varieties without labor- and time-intensive manual annotation.

</details>


### [159] [Capital Markets LLM Reliability Score (CM-LRS): From Plausible to Bankable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21340)
*Prerit Ahuja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 CM-LRS（资本市场 LLM 可靠性评分），从七个维度（事实准确性、证据可追溯性、数值一致性、工作流完整性、来源纪律、决策有用性、可审查性/可审计性）对 LLM 在资本市场工作流中的输出进行评分，并在五个工作流上对四个模型进行评测，发现闭源前沿模型评分相近，而开源基线显著落后，且差距集中在检索与合成而非提取环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估（如 FinanceBench、FinQA）仅在问答层面对模型进行评测，而资本市场实践者真正关心的是输出是否“可银行化”（即能在对手方或监管机构面前经得起推敲）。论文旨在填补这一评估空白，提供面向工作流输出层的可靠性评分。

Method: 定义 CM-LRS 框架，包含七个维度（事实准确性、证据可追溯性、数值一致性、工作流完整性、来源纪律、决策有用性、可审查性/可审计性），每个维度按 0-5 分制评分，依据受监管场景中审查者使用的信号构建评分标准；整体得分可根据工作流调整。在五个资本市场工作流（DCM 交易条款提取、先例检索、发行人概况综合、M&A 交易可比推理、ECM 交易条款提取）上，使用公开 SEC EDGAR 文件、英国收购公告及虚构合成数据，对四个模型（闭源：Sonnet 4.6、Opus 4.7、GPT-5.5；开源：Llama 3.3 70B）由四个独立 LLM 评审员（来自三个模型族）进行评分。

Result: ① 闭源前沿模型在四位评审员平均 CM-LRS 上差距极小（Sonnet 4.6=4.31，Opus 4.7=4.30，GPT-5.5=4.09），而开源基线 Llama 3.3 70B=3.15 均列末位；② 差距集中在检索（2.23）和综合（2.15）上，而非提取（0.84）；③ “决策有用性”维度跨模型差异最大（发行人概况上达 4.0 分），且评审员间一致性最高（平均 r=0.52）。

Conclusion: 单纯的语言流畅（plausibility）不足以支撑金融场景的严肃应用，“可银行化”（bankability）才是真正的标准。CM-LRS 为评估 LLM 在受监管资本市场工作流中的可靠性提供了一个多维、可调、可审计的评测框架，其发现表明提升模型在检索与综合能力上的表现比改进提取能力更为关键。

Abstract: In capital-markets workflows the question is rarely whether a large language model can produce a fluent draft, but whether the draft is bankable: defensible in front of a counter-party or a regulator, with the documents in hand. Existing methods address parts of that gap: open-domain QA benchmarks reward surface accuracy, and finance benchmarks (FinanceBench, FinQA, ConvFinQA) advance document-grounded and numerical QA but evaluate at the question-answer layer rather than the workflow outputs practitioners defend.
  We introduce CM-LRS, a Capital Markets LLM Reliability Score, evaluating outputs at the workflow-output layer across seven dimensions: factual accuracy, evidence traceability, numerical consistency, workflow completeness, source discipline, decision usefulness, and reviewability/auditability. Each is scored 0-5 against a rubric anchored on signals reviewers in regulated settings use; the aggregate is tunable to the workflow.
  We demonstrate CM-LRS on five workflows (DCM transaction-terms extraction, precedent retrieval, issuer profile synthesis, M&A transaction-comparable reasoning, ECM transaction-terms extraction) over public SEC EDGAR filings, a public UK takeover release, and fictional synthetic supplements, scoring four models against four independent LLM judges spanning three model families.
  Three findings. First, the frontier closed-source models cluster within 0.22 points on four-judge averaged CM-LRS (Sonnet 4.6 = 4.31, Opus 4.7 = 4.30, GPT-5.5 = 4.09); all four judges place the open-weights baseline (Llama 3.3 70B = 3.15) last. Second, that gap concentrates on retrieval (2.23) and synthesis (2.15), not extraction (0.84). Third, Decision Usefulness shows the widest cross-model dispersion of any dimension (4.0 points on issuer profiling) and top-tier inter-judge agreement (mean r = 0.52).
  Plausibility is cheap. Bankability is the bar.

</details>


### [160] [Word meaning co-determines vowel-inherent spectral change. A corpus-based investigation of conversational Mandarin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21391)
*Xiaoyun Jin,Mirjam Ernestus,R. Harald Baayen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过广义加性模型和分布语义词嵌入，研究发现自发性对话普通话中元音共振峰轨迹具有词特异性成分，且与语境中的词义相关，挑战了模块化语音生产认知模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究元音内在频谱变化（VISC）是否包含词汇语义信息，质疑传统模块化语音生产模型中语义与发音细节分离的观点。

Method: 使用广义加性模型（GAM）分析自发性对话普通话中的元音共振峰轨迹，结合分布语义学中的词嵌入（contextualized embeddings），控制元音时长、性别、说话人身份、协同发音、元音身份和话语位置等变量。

Result: F1和F2轨迹可从语境化词嵌入中预测，准确率显著高于随机基线；共振峰轨迹动态具有与语境中词义相关的词特异性成分。

Conclusion: 词汇的语义共同决定了其发音的精细细节，挑战了模块化认知模型，支持语音产生中语义与运动规划更紧密耦合的观点。

Abstract: This study investigates vowel-inherent spectral change (VISC) in spontaneous conversational Mandarin. Using the generalized additive model and word embeddings from distributional semantics, we show that, when controlling for variables such as vowel duration, gender, speaker identity, co-articulation, vowel identity, and utterance position, vowel formant trajectory dynamics have word-specific components that are tied to their meaning in context: The F1 and F2 trajectories of words can be predicted from their contextualized embeddings with an accuracy that substantially exceeds a permutation baseline. Challenging modular cognitive models of speech production, these results indicate that, words' semantics co-determine the fine details of their articulation.

</details>


### [161] [MemTools: A Unified Research Framework for Interoperable Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21404)
*Chengfeng Zhao,Jinhui Chen,Sirui Liang,Shizhu He,Yequan Wang,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemTools是一个基于互操作性的研究框架，通过解耦记忆系统组件与部署环境、标准化记忆生命周期、分离基准数据集与执行协议、以及提供统一的计算接口来协调符号、神经和多模态记忆表示，从而支持对智能体记忆设计变量的系统隔离与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体架构中记忆系统的研究因架构碎片化而受限，现有实现将记忆生命周期的不同阶段耦合在一起，评估逻辑与特定数据集纠缠不清，且对异构记忆类型的支持不足，亟需一个能够解耦、标准化和统一管理的框架来促进系统化研究。

Method: MemTools通过声明式数据契约标准化记忆生命周期实现组件的可互换组装；正交分离基准数据集与执行协议以支持受控评估；提供统一的计算接口在共享运行时中协调符号、神经和多模态记忆表示。

Result: 跨系统组件集成、评估协议重配置和异构记忆协调的实验证明，MemTools能够系统地隔离和分析记忆设计变量。

Conclusion: MemTools为推进智能体记忆的规范化研究提供了实用且可扩展的基础设施。

Abstract: While memory systems are essential for agent architectures, pervasive architectural fragmentation restricts systematic research. Existing implementations typically couple different stages of the memory lifecycle, entangle evaluation logic with specific datasets, and provide limited support for the management of heterogeneous memory types. We introduce MemTools, an interoperability research framework that decouples memory system components from their underlying deployment environments. MemTools standardizes the memory lifecycle through declarative data contracts, enabling the interchangeable assembly of components across different systems. It orthogonally separates benchmark datasets from execution protocols to facilitate controlled assessments. Furthermore, MemTools provides a unified computational interface for coordinating symbolic, neural, and multimodal memory representations within a shared runtime. Empirical evaluations on cross-system component integration, evaluation protocol reconfiguration, and heterogeneous memory coordination demonstrate that MemTools enables systematic isolation and analysis of memory design variables. These findings suggest that MemTools provides a practical and extensible infrastructure for advancing principled research on agent memory.

</details>


### [162] [Anti-Periodic Positional Encoding: Möbius Boundary Conditions Make In-Context Retrieval Reliable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21405)
*Ji Ho Bae*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于反周期频率梯度的旋转位置编码 Möbius RoPE，其位置和乐为 -1，使序列两端通过狄利克雷“偶极子”确定性耦合。实验表明，该编码在不改变混合模型困惑度的前提下，显著提升了长上下文单针检索的可靠性和稳定性，消除了检索性能的种子方差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 RoPE 的周期边界条件可能导致长上下文检索性能不稳定（种子方差大），亟需一种能提供确定性长程耦合的位置编码方案，在不牺牲语言建模性能的前提下提高检索可靠性。

Method: 在反周期频率梯级 θ_i=π(2i+1)/N 上构建旋转位置编码，使得每个旋转平面在训练上下文上推进奇数倍 π，位置和乐为 -1，序列两端通过闭合形式的狄利克雷“偶极子”耦合。在 160M 和 410M 参数规模的模型上进行预训练（2B FineWeb-Edu tokens），混合 25% 头使用 Möbius 频率，并与标准 RoPE、无位置编码（NoPE）以及周期/非周期对比实验进行对照。

Result: 混合模型困惑度不变（29.66 vs 29.72），但检索准确率大幅提升且方差降低（90.3±5.7% vs 63.3±31.4%），最差种子从 14% 提升至 86%。反周期梯级是效果的关键：非周期梯级无效，周期（和乐+1）梯级仅复现部分效果。将训练好的模型频率表换回标准 RoPE 后检索崩溃，且远距离 needles 受损最严重。NoPE 在短上下文更可靠但困惑度增加 13% 且外推最差。

Conclusion: Möbius RoPE 通过一行频率表替换即可为零成本提供检索“种子彩票”保险，在保持基线困惑度的同时显著提升单针检索的可靠性下限，且效果仅限于训练窗口内的检索任务。

Abstract: Möbius RoPE is a rotary positional encoding built on the anti-periodic frequency ladder $θ_i=π(2i+1)/N$: every rotation plane advances by an odd multiple of $π$ across the training context, so the positional holonomy is $-1$ and the two ends of the sequence are deterministically coupled through a closed-form Dirichlet "dipole"; to our knowledge this is the first anti-periodic boundary condition in positional encoding. We verify the theory numerically to $\sim 10^{-6}$ and pretrain 48 models spanning six 160M-class and three 410M-class arms (2B FineWeb-Edu tokens each; the hybrid arm puts Möbius frequencies on 25% of heads). Hybrid perplexity is unchanged (29.66 vs. 29.72), but needle-in-a-haystack retrieval becomes reliable: $90.3\pm5.7\%$ versus $63.3\pm31.4\%$ at context 512 ($n=6$ seeds), observed worst seed 86% versus 14%, robust variance tests $p=0.013$-$0.029$ (unadjusted), recurring at 410M (Levene $p=0.040$). Matched controls isolate the mechanism: an aperiodic ladder in the same frequency band reproduces none of the effect, and a periodic (holonomy $+1$) ladder only a fraction. Swapping trained models' frequency table back to standard RoPE (weights frozen) collapses retrieval, with damage concentrated on far needles: trained models depend on this long-range geometry. A NoPE arm is even more reliable at short context but pays a 13% perplexity tax and extrapolates worst; only the anti-periodic hybrid pairs baseline perplexity with a high reliability floor. The effect is scoped to single-needle retrieval within the training window; a one-line frequency swap thus provides zero-cost insurance against the retrieval seed lottery.

</details>


### [163] [An Evaluation Framework for Structured Audio Captions Validated by Controlled Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21424)
*Liang-Yuan Wu,Sripathi Sridhar,Mark Cartwright,Magdalena Fuentes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对结构化音频描述（AudioCards数据集）提出多轴评估框架，结合LLM语义评判与确定性声学度量，并通过受控扰动测试验证其能有效区分保留语义的改写与真正的语义/声学破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频描述评估指标仅适用于扁平文本输出，无法可靠评估音频描述中的多模态属性（声学与语义特性交织），亟需一个能全面评估结构化音频描述的多轴框架。

Method: 基于AudioCards数据集，沿五个正交轴（标签集、描述、逻辑推理、数值测量、频谱轮廓）进行评估；采用LLM评判器捕捉语义细微差别，结合确定性计算指标精确测量声学偏差；并引入受控扰动测试协议（注入类型化、分级错误）验证框架可靠性。

Result: 该框架能成功区分保留语义的改写与真正的语义及声学破坏，验证了其在结构化音频描述评估中的可靠性。

Conclusion: 提出的多轴评估框架克服了传统指标对结构化音频描述评估的局限性，通过正交轴分解和混合评估策略（LLM + 确定性指标）为AAC领域提供了更全面的评估方法。

Abstract: Recent advancements in automated audio captioning (AAC) have shifted from monolithic sentence generation toward structured formats that explicitly disentangle distinct acoustic and semantic properties. However, evaluating this heterogeneous data remains a significant challenge. Existing caption metrics focus on flat textual outputs and fail to reliably assess multimodal attributes. To bridge this gap, we propose a multi-axis evaluation framework tailored for structured audio descriptions. Building on the AudioCards dataset, we evaluate outputs across five orthogonal axes: tag-sets, descriptions, logical reasoning, numeric measurements, and spectral profiles. Our approach combines Large Language Model (LLM) judges to capture semantic nuance with deterministic computational metrics to precisely measure acoustic deviations. To rigorously validate the reliability of this framework, we introduce a controlled perturbation testing protocol that injects typed, graded errors into groundtruth annotations. Our results demonstrate that this framework successfully distinguishes meaning-preserving paraphrases from genuine semantic and acoustic corruptions.

</details>


### [164] [Token Budget Saturation and Mechanistic Early Detection of Reasoning Non-Convergence in Chain-of-Thought Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21433)
*Renuka Oladri,Niveda Jawahar,Abdirisak Mohamed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B等思维链推理模型中的双峰收敛现象：部分生成在token预算内终止（收敛）并达到90.3%准确率，而另一些则耗尽预算未能得出结论（非收敛，准确率仅6.6%）。通过在隐藏状态激活上训练线性探针，发现token 150处的第20层激活能以AUC 0.608提前区分收敛状态，优于基于熵和重复统计的行为基线。置换检验p=0.063，表明收敛命运部分编码在中间表征中，为早期退出推理和自适应计算分配提供了可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于揭示思维链推理模型中存在的双峰收敛现象，并探究是否能在生成早期通过内部模型表征检测到收敛结果，以便实现早期退出推理和自适应计算资源分配。

Method: 方法包括：1) 实证分析DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B在AIME 1983-2024上的生成行为，统计收敛率与准确率；2) 在token位置50-300的隐藏状态激活上训练线性探针（logistic regression），使用5折交叉验证评估AUC；3) 比较基于token熵和重复统计的行为基线；4) 进行sweep-level置换检验（100,000次置换）评估显著性。

Result: 结果显示：收敛生成在AIME上准确率90.3%，非收敛仅6.6%，总体收敛率62.0%；层20在token 150的激活探针AUC达到0.608（±0.080），即使在token 50也显著高于随机；激活探针始终优于行为基线；置换检验p=0.063，未达传统显著性阈值但表明存在微弱信号。

Conclusion: 结论是收敛命运在生成结束前已部分编码在中间层表征中，这一发现为早期退出推理和自适应计算分配提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought reasoning models such as DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B exhibit a bimodal convergence pattern: generations either terminate within a token budget (converged) or exhaust it without reaching a conclusion (non-converged). We characterize this phenomenon empirically, showing that converged generations achieve 90.3% accuracy on AIME 1983-2024 while non-converged ones achieve only 6.6%, with an overall convergence rate of 62.0%. We then ask whether this outcome is detectable early in the thinking chain using internal model representations. Training linear probes on hidden-state activations at token positions 50-300, we find that layer-20 activations at token 150 achieve AUC 0.608 (+-0.080, 5-fold CV), reliably above chance even at token 50. Activation probes consistently outperform behavioral baselines derived from token entropy and repetition statistics. A sweep-level permutation test yields p=0.063 (100,000 permutations), consistent with a modest signal that our sample size cannot confirm at conventional thresholds. These findings suggest that convergence fate is partially encoded in intermediate representations well before the generation ends, opening a path toward early-exit inference and adaptive compute allocation.

</details>


### [165] [When Trivia Is Not Trivial: Everyday Knowledge Failures in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21445)
*Anna Mosolova,Djamé Seddah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多语言常识问答基准 TriviaRoomQA，涵盖 288 个主题、3,300 道平行选择题（6 种欧洲语言）及 5,340 道法语附加题，用于评估大型语言模型在常识性、文化性和长尾知识上的表现。实验发现模型在历史、地理、数学等知识密集型领域表现强劲，但在流行文化（名人、音乐、电影、新闻）方面明显薄弱；同时相同问题在不同语言上的表现存在差异，说明事实性知识并非总是语言无关的。该数据集揭示了现有饱和学术基准未能捕获的重要知识缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学术基准已经趋于饱和，无法充分反映模型在日常常识、文化知识和长尾话题上的实际表现。问答节目、猜谜游戏等场景需要广泛而多样化的知识，但缺乏专门针对这类场景的多语言评测基准。本文旨在构建这样一个基准，系统性地揭示 LLM 在这些领域的知识缺口。

Method: 构建 TriviaRoomQA 数据集：包含 3,300 道平行多项选择题（覆盖 288 个主题，6 种欧洲语言）以及 5,340 道法语专属题。选取 30 个开源 LLM（参数量 7B–70B，来自欧洲、亚洲、北美的不同厂商），在统一的设置下进行评测，比较不同语言、不同知识领域上的性能差异。

Result: （1）模型在历史、地理、数学等知识密集型话题上表现强劲，但在名人、音乐、电影、新闻等流行文化话题上表现明显较弱。（2）即使对于同一道题，不同语言上的模型表现存在差异，表明事实知识并非完全语言无关。（3）现有学术饱和基准无法反映此类知识缺口，TriviaRoomQA 能够有效区分模型在不同知识领域的强弱。

Conclusion: LLM 在常识性、文化性和长尾知识方面存在显著不足，尤其是在流行文化领域；知识获取具有语言依赖性，多语言环境下模型的事实知识并不一致。TriviaRoomQA 作为新的评测工具，有助于引导未来工作关注这些被忽视的知识维度。

Abstract: Quiz rooms, trivia nights, and quiz shows challenge human knowledge across a wide range of topics, from canonical facts to everyday culture. In this paper, we examine whether large language models (LLMs) can perform competitively in such settings, using quiz-style questions to test them on both common and niche topics. We introduce TriviaRoomQA, a multilingual benchmark designed to evaluate everyday, culturally grounded, and long-tail knowledge across 288 topics. The benchmark contains 3,300 parallel multiple-choice questions in six European languages and additional 5,340 French-only questions for a more fine-grained case study. We evaluate 30 open-weight LLMs from European, Asian, and North American providers, covering models from 7 to 70B parameters. We find that models are strong on knowledge-intensive topics such as history, geography, and mathematics, but substantially weaker on everyday popular-culture topics such as celebrities, music, movies, and news. Moreover, model performance varies across languages even for the same underlying questions, suggesting that access to factual knowledge is not always language-independent. In sum, our dataset and experiments demonstrate an important knowledge gap which is not captured by existing academic-based saturated benchmarks.

</details>


### [166] [RUMBA: Russian User Memory Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21447)
*Elizaveta Shevtsova,Inna Glebkina,Mark Baushenko,Pavel Gulyaev,Alena Fenogenova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RUMBA是一个针对俄语和英语的长期对话记忆基准测试，提供细粒度的记忆问题分类和统一方法论，用于评估大语言模型在跨会话检索、组合和时间推理方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型长期记忆基准测试主要集中在英语，且依赖聚合检索指标，无法捕捉长程上下文、时间信息和推理之间的交互作用。因此需要一个新的基准来更细致地评估LLM的记忆能力。

Method: RUMBA包含带时间戳的用户-助手对话，以及需要跨会话检索、组合和推理的QA对。它提供了基于语义类型、会话范围、时间推理和时间表达明确性的细粒度问题分类法，并包含俄语和英语两个版本。

Result: 通过评估现有的记忆系统和长上下文模型，RUMBA能够作为诊断工具，分析模型在不同基准切片上的行为，识别不同记忆机制的优势和失败模式。

Conclusion: RUMBA填补了非英语长期记忆基准的空白，提供了更精细的评估维度，能够有效诊断LLM在长期对话记忆方面的能力。

Abstract: The ability to handle long-term memory in LLMs is becoming increasingly critical, yet existing benchmarks remain English-centric and rely on aggregate retrieval metrics, failing to capture interactions between long-range context, temporal information, and reasoning. To address this, we introduce RUMBA (Russian User Memory BenchmArk) - a new benchmark for long-term conversational memory that provides a fine-grained taxonomy of memory-centric question types and a unified methodology accounting for semantic type, session scope, temporal reasoning, and the explicitness of temporal expressions. RUMBA consists of timestamped user-assistant dialogues with QA pairs requiring retrieval, combination, and reasoning across sessions. While designed for Russian, we also provide an aligned English subset under the same methodology. We evaluate contemporary memory systems and long-context models, and show how RUMBA serves as a diagnostic tool to analyze model behavior across benchmark slices and identify strengths and failure modes of different memory mechanisms.

</details>


### [167] [What, Where, and How: Disentangling the Roles of Task, Language, and Model in Code Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21491)
*Piotr Wilam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过2×2实验设计（Python/Rust × Qwen2.5-Coder-7B/DeepSeek-Coder-V1-6.7B），研究独立训练的语言模型在代码表示上的普遍性。发现“什么概念获得电路”（What）在模型间一致（斯皮尔曼ρ≈0.65），“电路位置”（Where）和“电路增长方式”（How）由模型决定。普遍性是表示内容的属性，而非计算组织的属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究独立训练的语言模型是否以相同方式表示相同内容（即电路中概念的普遍性）。以往概念电路提取仅针对单一语言或单一模型，本文设计了最小的2×2结构（任务×语言×模型）来分离任务、语言和模型的影响。

Method: 将概念电路提取方法扩展到2×2设计：两种编程语言（Python、Rust）和两种模型（Qwen2.5-Coder-7B、DeepSeek-Coder-V1-6.7B）。对四种组合均以完全相同的方式测量了完整的语法概念清单（Python 58个，Rust 57个）。通过比对计算不同方面（What/Where/How）的相似性/差异性。

Result: ① What：模型就哪些概念获得专用电路达成一致（Python ρ=0.638，Rust ρ=0.673，均p<10⁻⁷）。② Where：Qwen在晚期层（~L17-19）处理概念，DeepSeek在早期层（L6-7），两种语言一致。③ How：Qwen给原子概念一个早期尖峰，DeepSeek没有。Rust概念获得的专用电路是Python的2-3倍。两个模型在语言间共享神经元（6/7和7/7配对结构），DeepSeek共享程度是Qwen的1.94倍。Qwen将Rust类型和特质机制的9个关键词绑定成一个紧密的神经元簇（Jaccard 0.535 vs 零模型0.112，p<0.001）。消融和线性探针证实电路具有功能。

Conclusion: “电路是否普遍”没有单一答案：对What（内容）是肯定的，对Where和How（组织方式）是否定的。普遍性是表示内容的属性，而非计算组织的属性。所有结论限于此2×2设计，下一步是检验每个模型的特征是否能预测第三个模型。

Abstract: Do independently trained language models come to represent the same thing in the same way? We answer for code, extending a recently introduced concept-circuit extraction method to a 2x2 design -- Python and Rust crossed with Qwen2.5-Coder-7B and DeepSeek-Coder-V1-6.7B -- and measuring a complete inventory of grammatical concepts (58 Python, 57 Rust) identically in all four cells: the smallest design that separates what depends on the task, the language, and the model.
  The answer splits into three parts. What earns dedicated circuitry is set by the task: the models agree on which concepts receive circuits (Spearman $ρ$ = 0.638 for Python, 0.673 for Rust, both p < $10^{-7}$). Where those circuits sit is set by the model: Qwen processes concepts in a late band (~L17-19), DeepSeek at L6-7, for both languages. How circuits grow across layers is also set by the model: Qwen gives its atomic concepts an early spike that DeepSeek does not. "Are circuits universal?" thus has no single answer: yes for What, no for Where and How -- universality is a property of representational content, not of computational organisation.
  None of this structure was fixed in advance. The agreement could have landed anywhere between independence and identity; it lands at $ρ\approx 0.65$. Rust constructs receive 2-3x more concept-specific circuitry than their Python equivalents, in both models. Both models share neurons between the languages (6/7 and 7/7 paired constructs), DeepSeek 1.94x more than Qwen -- a direction no prior result predicts. And Qwen binds nine keywords of Rust's type-and-trait machinery into one tight neuron cluster (Jaccard 0.535 vs null 0.112, p < 0.001), a semantic dimension invisible in surface syntax. Ablation and linear probes confirm the circuits are functional.
  All claims are scoped to this 2x2; whether the per-model profile predicts a third model is the designed next test.

</details>


### [168] [Artificial Epanorthosis: Why large language models overuse a classical rhetorical figure, and how to mitigate it](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21498)
*Federico Boggia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大型语言模型（LLM）中频繁出现的修辞格“epanorthosis”（自我修正，如“这不是一门课程。这是一场变革之旅”）。作者认为，这种过度使用是一种训练得来的倾向，主要源于训练数据中充斥的宣传性文本以及偏好调优（RLHF）对自信、强调性表达的奖励；从左到右的自回归生成机制只是放大器而非根本原因。论文提出了一个评估框架，通过“Epanorthosis指数”（相对于人类基准的密度）来衡量该修辞格在不同文体中的使用情况。对三个规模的指令调优模型家族的初步测量发现，模型在演说文体中过度使用（意大利语中接近三倍），在非正式问答中不足，而在议论文、新闻和百科全书式散文中与人类相当。论文还探讨了缓解技术，包括轻量级LoRA适配器、单行指令可将该修辞格减少一半至四分之三，以及监督微调适配器几乎完全消除它，并可通过缩放系数回调至人类水平。最终目标不是消除，而是使模型在不同文体中与人类使用率校准。论文指出，真正的风险是人类开始像机器一样写作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型生成的文本中系统性地出现了一种古典修辞格epanorthosis（自我修正），这种过度使用可能导致人类写作风格被机器同化。现有研究缺乏对这种修辞现象的系统量化分析及其与人类基准的校准，因此需要建立评估方法和缓解策略。

Method: 1. 提出Epanorthosis指数（密度相对于人类使用率）来量化该修辞格。2. 对三个规模的指令调优模型家族进行初步测量，比较不同文体（演说、问答、议论文、新闻、百科）中的使用情况。3. 调查缓解技术：轻量级LoRA适配器、单行指令、监督微调适配器，并引入缩放系数进行校准。

Result: 模型在演说文体中过度使用epanorthosis（意大利语中近三倍），在非正式问答中不足，在议论文、新闻和百科散文中与人类相当。单行指令可将该修辞格减少一半至四分之三，监督微调适配器几乎完全消除它，缩放系数可回调至人类水平。

Conclusion: 目标不是消除epanorthosis，而是使其在不同文体中与人类使用率校准。真正的风险是人类开始像机器一样写作，因此需要保持人类修辞风格的多样性。

Abstract: A rhetorical figure that Cicero and Quintilian catalogued two thousand years ago reappears, systematically, in the text of large language models: epanorthosis, the self-correction of the specimen «This is not a course. It is a journey of transformation». This essay argues that the overuse is a trained disposition, driven mainly by a training distribution rich in promotional prose and by preference tuning (RLHF) that rewards confident, emphatic phrasing; the left-to-right nature of generation is an amplifier rather than the root cause. Building on evidence that models diverge from human rhetorical style, and on Fontanier's classification of epanorthosis as a figure of thought, it sets out a programme that scores the figure against genre-specific human baselines through an Epanorthosis Index (density relative to the human rate). A first measurement, on three sizes of one instruction-tuned model family, finds mis-calibration by register in both directions: the models overshoot in oratory (about twofold, near threefold in Italian, concentrated in the larger tiers) and undershoot in informal question-and-answer writing, while matching humans in argument, journalism, and encyclopedic prose. Three constructive contributions follow: a survey of mitigation techniques centred on lightweight LoRA adapters; a demonstration, in Italian, that a one-line instruction cuts the figure by half to nearly three-quarters and that a supervised-fine-tuning adapter removes it almost entirely, with a scaling coefficient that dials the reduction back onto the human rate; and the argument that the target is calibration to the human rate for each genre, not elimination. It closes on the stakes: the real risk is that we begin to write like the machines.

</details>


### [169] [DONDO: Open w2v-BERT Speech-Recognition Base Models for African Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21540)
*Paul Azunre*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DONDO 是一个基于 w2v-BERT 2.0 的开放许可自动语音识别（ASR）基础模型系列，包含 21 个单语模型和 5 个多语模型，覆盖非洲 6 国 27 种语言变体。通过两/三步学习率退火微调流程和轻量级语言条件注入机制，多语模型在保持单检查点覆盖多语言的同时，平均词错误率（WER）达到 10-13%，接近单语模型性能。所有模型以 Apache-2.0 许可证发布在 Hugging Face KhayaAI 组织下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲语言缺乏足够的转录语音数据，现有 ASR 模型覆盖有限。作者旨在构建开放、许可宽松的 ASR 基础模型，利用宗教文本中朗读语音（许可清晰、正字法一致）作为训练数据，填补非洲语言语音识别的空白。

Method: 采用 w2v-BERT 2.0 自监督语音编码器，在朗读宗教语音上微调。提出两/三步学习率退火微调流程：先以高学习率适应共享多语模型，再退火恢复（甚至超越）强单语基线。设计轻量级语言条件机制：将 one-hot 语言身份作为前缀帧注入声学特征，推理时引导多语检查点指向目标语言。

Result: 构建了 21 个单语模型和 5 个多语模型，覆盖 27 种语言变体。退火多语模型平均 WER 为 10-13%，基本缩小了与单语模型的差距。覆盖语言拥有约 1 亿第一语言使用者，加上第二语言使用者则更多。所有模型在 Apache-2.0 许可下发布。

Conclusion: DONDO 展示了利用宗教文本音频和退火微调策略可有效扩展 ASR 到低资源非洲语言。轻量级语言条件机制使单一多语检查点灵活服务于多种语言，兼顾覆盖率与性能。开放许可发布促进了商业和学术进一步微调与应用。

Abstract: We present DONDO, a family of open, permissively licensed automatic speech recognition (ASR) base models for African languages, built on the w2v-BERT 2.0 self-supervised speech encoder. DONDO comprises twenty-one monolingual models and five multilingual models spanning twenty-seven language varieties across Ghana, Sierra Leone, Nigeria, Senegal, Kenya and Zimbabwe. Models are fine-tuned primarily on read speech drawn from religious texts, which offer broad, license-clear and orthographically consistent coverage for languages that otherwise lack transcribed audio. We describe a two-step (and, for one family, three-step) learning-rate-annealed fine-tuning procedure that first adapts a shared multilingual model at a high learning rate and then anneals it to recover, and in several cases surpass, strong monolingual baselines. We further describe a lightweight language-conditioning mechanism that injects a one-hot language identity as a sequence of prefix frames prepended to the acoustic features, allowing a single multilingual checkpoint to be steered to a target language at inference. Across the five multilingual families the annealed models reach average word error rates (WER) of 10-13%, closing most of the gap to monolingual models while covering many languages in a single checkpoint. All models are released on the Hugging Face KhayaAI organisation under the Apache-2.0 license (attribution only) so that others may fine-tune them freely, including for commercial use.
